- **ndv-breakdown**: Chia GDD thành danh sách feature + thứ tự build. Đọc cây heading của index (không đọc toàn văn), gom section thành feature, đề xuất depends_on và milestone, phân loại section không thuộc feature nào → &lt;ndvRoot&gt;/features.json + breakdown.md. Không sinh card. Gate: user duyệt danh sách. --incremental: chỉ thêm feature cho section mới. --brownfield: gắn đường dẫn code cho từng feature. ([eternalTornado/ndv-kit](https://github.com/eternalTornado/ndv-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/ndv-breakdown))
- **ndv-card**: Viết card cho một feature (WHAT, không HOW) ngay trước khi làm nó: đọc đúng các section GDD trong sources, câu hỏi/quyết định liên quan, card của dependency → &lt;ndvRoot&gt;/features/&lt;F&gt;-&lt;slug&gt;/card.md với REQ [ready|assumed: QID|blocked: QID] + scenario Given/When/Then có level test. Gate: check-card + user duyệt. --delta: GDD đổi / GD trả lời / thiếu scenario → changes/Cnn/delta.md. --from-code: feature đã có code (brownfield). ([eternalTornado/ndv-kit](https://github.com/eternalTornado/ndv-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/ndv-card))
- **ndv-contextual**: Reader-modeling cognitive module. Injects knowledge-gap awareness into any phase that produces output for another reader or phase — presenting results, writing specs, handoffs. Generalized from ndv-explain (Patient). ([emb715/neurodiveragents](https://github.com/emb715/neurodiveragents/tree/HEAD/skills/ndv-contextual) · ★2)
- **ndv-direct**: Zero-filler communication cognitive module. Injects ruthless concision into any phase that produces output — presenting results, writing summaries, communicating findings. - **nearest-neighbor-chain**: Greedy nearest-neighbor chain decomposition: convert a pairwise similarity matrix into an ordered list of variable-length chains (semantic threads) by walking the sorted pair list and extending chains only at their endpoints. ([thistleknot/skills](https://github.com/thistleknot/skills/tree/HEAD/nearest-neighbor-chain))
- **nearest-neighbor-cluster-label-mapping**: Use when synthetic single-cell observations lack labels and you need ground-truth cluster assignments from a reference dataset to compute clustering-quality metrics such as the Davies–Bouldin index or adjusted Rand index. - **nearest-neighbor-clustering-for-mass-spectrometry**: Use when you have extracted mass tracks (EICs) from individual samples at 0.001 amu m/z resolution and need to align them into a composite mass grid for feature detection. - **nearest-neighbor-cross-modality-label-regression**: Use when you have aligned latent embeddings of two single-cell modalities and need a quantitative downstream benchmark of alignment quality via cross-modality value prediction (Spearman correlations), complementing Leiden-clustering ARI. - **nearest-neighbor-distance-analysis**: Use when you must score how hard each cohort sample is to infer from representatives — e.g. comparing active-learning vs passive selection at equal representative counts, or deciding how many representatives are needed for a bulk cohort. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/nearest-neighbor-distance-computation))

- **nearest-neighbor-gaussian-process-fitting**: Use when you need to rank spatially variable genes in a spatial transcriptomics dataset (SpatialExperiment or coordinates + logcounts matrix), especially with thousands+ spots or within a priori defined spatial domains, and require a flexible per-gene length scale parameter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/nearest-neighbor-gaussian-process-fitting))
- **nearest-neighbor-graph-construction**: Use when you need a cell-cell similarity network whose neighbor count adapts to local neighborhood density and heterogeneity (e.g., after computing multilevel archetype encodings H*), without pre-specifying k. - **nearest-neighbor-graph-construction-pynndescent**: Use when when analyzing spatial molecular data (e.g., from tissue sections or microscopy) where you need to build k-nearest neighbor graphs on high-dimensional coordinate arrays (n_obs × n_dims) and exact nearest neighbor computation is too slow or memory-intensive; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/nearest-neighbor-graph-construction-pynndescent))
- **nearest-neighbor-graph-construction-scrnaseq**: Use when an annotated scRNA-Seq dataset processed through FindVariableFeatures/ScaleData/RunPCA needs a k-nearest-neighbor cell graph with vertex attributes (e.g. age, disease severity) before community-based differential abundance testing. - **nearest-neighbor-graph-smoothing**: Use when scoring sparse, noisy scRNA-seq count data with bulk-style gene set methods (e.g. singscore, ssGSEA, summed_up) and per-cell signal is too weak — e.g. signature genes missed or AUC degraded by downsampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/nearest-neighbor-hit-miss-weighting))
- **nearest-neighbor-index-construction**: Use when when you have millions of high-resolution MS/MS spectra converted to low-dimensional vectors (via feature hashing) and need to compute a sparse pairwise distance matrix for downstream density-based clustering. - **nearest-neighbor-search-comparison-profiling**: Use when benchmarking whether ANN indexing (feature hashing + Voronoi inverted index) avoids exhaustive pairwise spectrum comparison at scale, e.g. on millions of MS/MS spectra from proteomics PRIDE datasets. - **nearest-neighbor-sequence-identity-search**: Use when you need to explain a classifier's per-sequence errors by distance to training data: bucket held-out 16S sequences by nearest-neighbor percent identity (&gt;97%, 90–97%, &lt;90%) to stratify F1 degradation. - **nearest-neighbour-distance-computation**: Use when building the distance layer of a rare-cell detection pipeline: you have a large scRNA-seq expression matrix and need per-cell nearest-neighbour distances (D&lt;m,k&gt;) feeding skewness&gt;k&gt;2 gap statistics. - **nearest-neighbour-overcorrection-assessment**: Use when benchmarking cross-species (or multi-dataset) integration strategies and you need to check whether integrated embeddings preserve distinguishability of cell types present in only a subset of input datasets, as species count increases. - **nearest-spd-matrix-projection**: Use when an empirical M-by-M pairwise distance-correlation matrix of metabolite features must be made non-negative-definite before eigen-analysis-based effective-number-of-tests (Meff) correction in a MWAS multiple-testing pipeline.
